# Adjutant v2 requirement traceability

Current working record, 2026-09-22. The product remains under implementation. States apply to individual requirements; simulated provider responses do not establish live integration. Empty execution-path fields are not evidence of completion.

State counts: {'FAIL': 370, 'PASS': 28, 'BLOCKED_EXTERNAL_AUTH': 91}. Each of the ten platforms has all 25 requested operations traced separately.
